# M5 per-feature gate cards (for the 13:15 CET joint plan, D-CEO-20261030-06/07)
Ranking: m5-value-ranking.md rev 2 (47ba604e, CEO-approved). Owners: PMC (Collection section), PMW (Workers section), Econ (post-L10).

## Card template (one per feature)
- Spec: path + sha
- Art spec sha1, from art-ux/decisions.md. If there's no signed flow, write "Art flow pending, NOT gate-ready".
- ACs: Given/When/Then, numbered <FEATURE>-AC1..n
- UI check: screenshots at 360/390/1280 on all 3 engines, checked against the Art sha
- AC7 row: P95 ≤180 s on the shipped CSV
- Open spec gaps
- QA line (HoQA adds): cases, engines, effort

Rule (D-CEO-20261030-07): a flag goes to 1 only when its row is built and its gate has passed.

## Collection (PMC)
Features: streak UI + tapCoins, golden, breeds (Art f349f29f), broody, zeroGainUi (Art 14ec8850)

  - **STK-AC2** (ST-AC29). Given only `tapCoins` = 1, when the player taps, then coins are paid, s = 0 and no meter shows. Given only `streak` = 1, then s builds and no coins are paid.

  - **STK-AC4** (ST-AC3, ST-AC5). Given both flags on at F ≥ 8, when the player taps at 10/s for 10 s, then credited = 10 ±1 and s = 0.10 ±0.01. Given s = 0.27 (the v5 cap; ship-set, D-HOP-27) and no taps, then s holds for 3.0 s, falls 0.03/s, and `streak_peak` fires once.
  - **STK-AC5** (ST-AC7, ST-AC8). Given s = x, when any egg is sold, then it credits `round(k×base×g×(1+x))`. Golden cash, tap coins and the Broody nest are unaffected by s.
  - **STK-AC6** (ST-AC21, ST-AC25, ST-AC26b §9.1 csv). Given s = 0.187, then the fill is 69.3% (**PROVISIONAL**: PMC's own arithmetic, 0.187/0.27, pending Econ's 0.27 run (Sat 12:00 streak X1) and streak spec errata v1.3.3 (due Sat 31 Oct 14:00 CET), D-HOP-2026-10-30-31; spec ST-AC21 says 66.8% at 0.28) and reads `+18% price`. At the cap 0.27 (ship-set, D-HOP-27) it shows a gold `MAX +27%` with no pulse. Rects are ±1 px of the §9.1 csv.

Given Sound on, when s goes from 0 to the cap 0.27 (ship-set, D-HOP-27), then there are exactly 4 tier pop-ups (the tier thresholds at 0.27 are **PROVISIONAL** pending Econ's 0.27 run (Sat 12:00 streak X1) and streak spec errata v1.3.3 (due Sat 31 Oct 14:00 CET), D-HOP-2026-10-30-31) and pitch steps and 1 cap chime. Muted: none.
  - **STK-AC8** (ST-AC14, ST-AC30). Given a Coop Hand collect, welcome-back Collect, a synthetic click, a Broody collect or a flutter tap, then there is no credited tap, no change to s and no coins.

      - it has no ring, countdown or progress element (no `conic-gradient` or progress role), and `getAnimations().length === 0` while pending;
      - there is no `aria-label`, and the accessible name is "+3% paused";
      - the `aria-describedby` text is exactly "Breed bonus paused while you tap fast. Returns when you tap slower.".

    - **7f, return pop** (BR-AC52, BR-AC52b). Given ≥ 1 settled Present breed, W 10 / k 6, wall time = focus time, and counted taps at focus s 0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, then 10.5, 11.5, 12.5, 13.5, 14.5, repeated from focus s 30, when the run plays under Playwright `page.clock`, then:

      - on every tick, the chip's border, strike, accessible name and `aria-describedby` text, and the slot-card mirror's strike, match the gate with no lag;
      - there are exactly 2 return pops, at focus s 10 and 40, each a 300 ms scale peaking ≤ 1.08 (none under reduced motion, none on load or settle);
      - in any 10,000 ms span at most 1 pop starts: a return at t0 + 9,999 ms is an instant swap, and one at t0 + 10,000 ms pops.
    - **7g, tooltip** (BR-AC52). Given the HUD chip in either state, when it is tapped, then:
      - it shows exactly "Bonus returns when you tap slower" at HUD bottom + 8 px for 1.6 s, and not while a `.toast` shows;
      - stamps, streak, `credited_taps` and `taps_total` are unchanged;
      - hit areas are ≥ 44 × 44 px.

Features: pause, refill (Art signed m5-refill-flows v1.0.1 43828d0b), abilities (Art flow v0.1 bbe9d142 drafted, sign due Sat 31 Oct 12:00 CET), clockWage. fatigueCue handed to PM Market (D-HOP-2026-10-30-16 (2)).

PM, Workers, 2026-10-30 12:27 CET; revised 2026-10-30 12:31 CET (D-HOP-2026-10-30-16; D-PMW-2026-10-30-07). Paper only. Revised 2026-10-30 13:17 CET (D-PMW-2026-10-30-10; D-HOE-20261030-17/-18): rc2 re-pinned to v1.3.1, pause to v1.3.11, telemetry rows RF-AC12–14 and PAU-AC13 added, gap 9 closed. Revised 2026-10-30 13:20 CET (D-HOP-2026-10-30-35/-36): rc2 re-pinned to v1.3.2, Boost slot visible in v5 (RF-AC15), first-hire nudge deferred to 5.1. Revised 2026-10-30 13:21 CET: rc2 re-pinned to v1.3.3 (entry `fatigue_chip`, RF-AC10). Telemetry is built in v5 (D-CEO-20261030-09), so these rows are not paper-only.
- Every sha1 below was checked with `sha1sum` on disk at 12:20–12:30 CET. Only live files are cited, never the frozen `<name>.<8hex>.md` copies.
- Feature numbers come from `product/m5-value-ranking.md` rev 2, cited as **bfdfd73e** (the current file). 47ba604e is the version the CEO approved; the only change since is the pause cite moving to v1.3.10 (D-HOP-2026-10-30-16 (5); D-HOP-2026-10-30-14).
- The v5 RC ships every `m4.flags.*` at 0 (D-HOP-2026-10-30-10 amendment, D-CEO-20261030-06). "Flag-off" means `m4.enabled` = 1 and this feature's flag = 0.
- **Art sha note:** `art-ux/decisions.md` records the Pause and clock-wage sign-offs by date and file, not by sha1. The Art sha1s below are on-disk hashes that PMW computed. Art to confirm.
- **AC7 definition (applies to every card).** AC7 is the studio pacing gate run by the two pacing bots (`tests/bot.ts` and `scratch/m4-ac7-bot`), which buy the game's own best-payback pick. Seeds 1–200. Both bots must agree within ±20% on the same SHA (decisions.md, D-HOP-2026-10-19 bot ruling). Under D-CEO-20261019-04 it gates Farm-level pacing: L10 P95 ≤12.1 min, plus the L30+ step ratio. The "P95 ≤180 s" row is gates.md E-G4: the longest stretch with no affordable purchase, P95 across seeds (worst seed is reported). It applies to every RC, flags on or off (D-HOP-2026-10-30-06 (1)). It is measured at P95 on every row, and worst seeds over 180 s are listed, not blocking (D-HOP-2026-10-30-08 (2)). Profiles: optimal, seller15, casual. AC7/X1 must read the shipped CSV and record its hash in the results (D-HOP-2026-10-30-07 amendment, gate 2). ~~Nowhere does it say which bot behaviour (pause pattern, refill policy, call policy) a per-feature flag-on AC7 row should use, so each card below proposes one and lists it as a gap.~~ **Ruled (D-HOP-2026-10-30-16 (4)):** Econ owns the flag-on AC7 bot configs (pause pattern, refill policy, call policy) and pins them in one doc (ETA after the 19:30 golden deliverable). The configs each card proposes are input. No flag-on AC7 row can run until that doc's sha is on the card.

Given `pause` = 0 and the fixture `tests/fixtures/saves/m4-exp1-committed-unseen.json` (file sha256 `de6b0297…7961`, save-string sha256 `8889e669…a9ff`), when it runs through validate → m5Load → serialize with the pinned `NOW = Date.UTC(2026, 9, 30, 9)` and no `saveSeq`, then the output is byte-identical to the pinned comparison save. **BLOCKED** (see gap 1).
  - **PAU-AC3** (AC-P1 (b)). Given `pause` = 0 and a committed (tracksV7) save, when Game boot runs plus `save()` with a reload gap under 60 s, then the saved `state` is byte-identical to the comparison save's (`savedAt` and `saveSeq` excluded). It passed on the fixture (Saves QA acp1-a923206).
  - **PAU-AC4** (AC-P2). Given the flag is on, at F9 there is no toggle. At F10 there is exactly one toggle per hired worker.
  - **PAU-AC5** (AC-P3, AC-P4). Given a paused MH or CH, over 120 s of sim, then it performs 0 actions and pays 0 wage, and the Hawker demand multiplier reads 1.00. Given a Grader at tier 2 (×2.0), `graderMult` = 1.00 when paused, 1.15 when Asleep and unpaused, and 2.00 when Working and unpaused.
  - **PAU-AC6** (AC-P5, AC-P6, AC-P7). Given a worker at 20%, after 360 s paused, then it's at 30.0% ±0.1. At 100% it stays at 100.0 and stays paused. Given `cdS` = 0.7 s, after 60 s paused and then Resume, the first action comes 0.7 s ±0.1 after Resume. Taps 1.5 s apart give 1 state change; taps 2.1 s apart give 2.
  - **PAU-AC7** (AC-P8, AC-P9). Given a call with 20 s left, when the worker is paused, then the effect ends in ≤1 tick, `ability_ended{cause:"paused"}` is logged, and `readyAt` is unchanged. Given Rush and Shout are ready, after an MH Resume both are `aria-disabled` for 44.9 s, reading [icon-pause] "0:ss" counting down from 0:45. At 45.1 s both are enabled. A CH Resume causes no lockout. The B5/P-13 reload, hide and menu clauses apply.
  - **PAU-AC8** (AC-P10, AC-P11, AC-P12). Given an Unpaid MH with coins below 1 min of wage, Pause then Resume leaves it Unpaid. Given 15 Pause/Resume pairs, one toggle every 2.0 s over 60 s (a sim test may toggle faster), the MH makes no more actions than an unpaused MH over the same unpaused time (±1), and every action logs its wage (AC-P11 v1.3.16). Given a paused worker at 40%, Manage refills it to 100 and resumes it. Manage all with 1 paused worker and 1 unpaused tired worker refills only the unpaused one, and N reads 1.
  - **PAU-AC9** (AC-P13, save round-trip). Given the hired-worker tracksV7 fixture (gap 2), on the real hide/show path:
    - a 59.9 s hidden gap while paused gives +1.66% ±0.05;
    - hidden gaps of 60.0 s and 600 s give +0;
    - after a reload the worker is still paused, with energy ±0.1 and `callLockoutS` ±0.5 s;
    - an rc1 save loads with no `paused: true`, and SAVE_VERSION is unchanged;
    - E9: a pre-Pause-build save holding `paused: true` loads paused;
    - E13 steps (1)–(3): with the flag at 0, `state` is byte-identical on a gap under 60 s and the pause fields are preserved; switching the flag back to 1 re-pauses the worker with no recovery credited; `callLockoutS` above the cap reads `tc_pause_call_lockout_s`, and a negative value reads 0.
  - **PAU-AC10** (AC-P14, sim). Given G5-P patterns (a), (b), (d), (e), (f) and (g), then each falls inside its §10 band. G1 holds against the X1 oracle (economy.md §13.2a, seeds 1–200: ≤2.00 P95, ≤2.25 worst, ±20% band). The L0–9 trace is byte-identical with the flag on and off.
  - **PAU-AC11** (AC-P15, AC-P17, a11y/layout). At 360×640 and 1280:
    - the toggle is ≥44×44 px and ≥8 px from every other interactive box;
    - the paused card is 128 ±1 px;
    - the pill reads "Paused" on neutral grey, and the pill, sub-line and buttons are all ≥4.5:1 contrast;
    - there are 0 yawn/asleep overlays;
    - the sub-line reads exactly "On break · no wage · recovering" at 40%;

  - **PAU-AC12** (AC-P18). Given `pause` = 1, the CH paused and the coop full for 120 s, then there is no Pause-specific coop-full UI, and the ring holds 0 `coop_full_start` events and 0 `coop_hand_state` fields.
  - **PAU-AC13** (§12 v1.3.11; D-PMW-2026-10-30-10; D-HOE-20261030-18). Given `pause` = 1 and `rc2Refill` = 0, each Pause, Resume and Manage-resume emits exactly one event (`worker_paused` or `worker_resumed{cause: manual|manage}`) and 0 `rest_toggled`. Given `rc2Refill` = 1, it emits exactly one `rest_toggled` and 0 `worker_paused`/`worker_resumed`. Same assert as RF-AC12.

- **ACs** (rows marked † follow Art signed m5-refill-flows v1.0.1 43828d0b; **Required markup (Art v1.0.1 §8.2, RF-UI-9/19/20; `art-ux/reviews/m5-refill-axe/sr-markup.md`; rc2 v1.3.6):** one inline `.lbl` wrapper with an inline `.sr-in{font-size:0;line-height:0;color:transparent}` suffix, never `position:absolute`, no `aria-label`: `<span class="lbl">Refill<span class="sr-in">, {Role}</span></span>` (enabled/Unpaid), `<span class="lbl">Next charge in<span aria-hidden="true" data-cd=" 2:30"></span><span class="sr-in"> progress, {Role}</span></span>` (0 charges), `<span class="lbl">Rested, refill below 50%<span class="sr-in">, {Role}</span></span>` (Rested). The v0.3.19 `.sr` direct-flex-child markup fails RF-UI-9/19/20 (Chromium AX / Playwright give `Refill , {Role}`); failure-cost v0.3.21 `f961a73403726f7bcf6c81c2ed13913f0de260d7` carries the fix (D-PMW-2026-10-30-15).):
  - **RF-AC1** (AC-FC16 (b)). Given `rc2Refill` = 0, `clockWage` = 0 and `autoRefill` = 0, then sim traces are byte-identical to the goldens flag-off `f90d1678…` and M4-on `a54c9cf9…` (`flagoff-golden.a01faea.test.ts` / `m4on-golden.90a9c84.test.ts` plus storage pins). The 30 s `manageCdS` behaves as in 30b29d3, and no rc2 state, save or event field is written.
  - **RF-AC2** (AC-RF1 v1.1; D-HOP-2026-10-30-13). Given the flag is on, when scanned at 360, 1024 (docked) and 1280, then no element with role button or link whose accessible name matches /refill/i exists outside the Shift sheet and the nav button. The worker card holds [pause][Manage][call] only, and Manage opens the Shift sheet focused on that worker.
  - **RF-AC3** (AC-RF2) †. Given a Refillable worker and ≥1 charge, a refill restores exactly one worker to 100% and uses 1 charge. Given 0 charges:
    - the Refill button is disabled with `aria-disabled="true"` only;
    - it reads "Next charge in m:ss" (e.g. "Next charge in 2:30"), updating every 1 s;
    - its markup is the #83 ba398b6 build in fixed DOM order: text "Next charge in", then an empty text-free `<span aria-hidden="true" data-cd="m:ss">` drawn by `::after{content:attr(data-cd)}` (updated each second, rounded up, never 0:00), then the inline `.sr-in` span " progress, {Role}" inside the one `.lbl` wrapper (Art v1.0.1 §8.2; was the `.sr` position:absolute pattern, superseded); no `aria-label`; its accessible name is "Next charge in progress, {Role}" (no digits) (failure-cost v0.3.21 `f961a73403726f7bcf6c81c2ed13913f0de260d7` (was v0.3.19 `4f5bbc5c`), rc2 v1.3 `2fdd84a2`; D-HOP-2026-10-30-19 (2) as amended 12:39).
    This replaces the spec's "{ceil(s)}s" and "disabled attribute" wording. Given a worker above 50%, the button is disabled and reads "Rested, refill below 50%" (name "Rested, refill below 50%, {Role}"). Enabled, and the Unpaid row (`aria-disabled`, no countdown; FC-N1): "Refill" + `.sr-in` ", {Role}" (name "Refill, {Role}"); Rested likewise via `.sr-in`. Names come from content only; the button has no `aria-label`. Bar: axe `label-content-name-mismatch` = 0 for all 3 states (0 charges, Rested, enabled) in Chromium, WebKit and Firefox at 360/390/1280; the computed name has no digit in any state (CDP getFullAXTree + Playwright ariaSnapshot) and the button has no `aria-label` attribute.
  - **RF-AC4** (AC-RF2, v1.2 decisions 1, 2, 3 and 8; save round-trip). Charges come back +1 every 150 s ±1 s, up to 2. Given offline time or a hide of ≥60 s, then charges and the regen timer are frozen; a hide under 60 s is simulated normally. A visible tab that stalls 60 s or more simulates the first 60 s, then freezes (game.ts:425, D-HOE-20 G-5). Charge regen follows the same rule. (rc2 v1.3.10.) Partial regen progress round-trips through save/load. Starting charges are 2 for a new game, at first hire, and when loading an rc1 save. No SAVE_VERSION bump.
  - **RF-AC5** (AC-RF3; v1.2 decision 5). Rows are sorted by `workerAtRisk` descending; ties go to lower energy, then W1→W4. The shown value matches debug within ±$0.01. Rows re-sort on open and every 5 s, never within 1 s of a tap.
  - **RF-AC6** (AC-RF6; v1.2 decision 4). Given a Resting worker, including an Asleep one, with 0 charges, it goes from 0% to 100% in 3600 s ±2 s. While it rests, MH/CH timers are frozen, no wage is charged and the Grader bonus is 0. Rest ends on its own at 100% with cause `auto_full`.
  - **RF-AC7** (AC-FC16 (a)). Given the flag on, charges 2, and MH and CH both at or below the threshold, when MH is refilled at tick k, then CH's Refill is enabled at tick k+1 and a tap refills it (charges 2 → 1 → 0). There is no 30 s lockout and no "Ready in" caption.
  - **RF-AC8** (D-HOP-2026-10-30-13 condition; failure-cost AC-FC20 v0.3.24 `54b994bddc479b5d114e02dbebed04813f007196` (re-pinned 14:20 CET, D-PMW-2026-10-30-25; freeze v0.3.23 `8c03eb07`; was v0.3.21 `f961a734`, v0.3.19 `4f5bbc5c`)) †. Given the flag on and a worker in each state (Refillable 30–50%, Tired 1–29%, Asleep, Unpaid, Paused), then its card shows that state's existing label/cue (no new asset), no Rested caption, no countdown, and no element with role button or link named /refill/i. When Manage is activated, the Shift sheet opens focused on that worker's row. At 0 charges the row button reads "Next charge in m:ss" (`aria-disabled`; markup is the #83 ba398b6 build in fixed DOM order: text "Next charge in", then an empty text-free `<span aria-hidden="true" data-cd="m:ss">` drawn by `::after{content:attr(data-cd)}` (updated each second, rounded up, never 0:00), then a visually hidden `.sr` span " progress, {Role}" (`.sr` = `src/ui/styles.css:169`, position:absolute clip pattern, not a flex item); no `aria-label`; name "Next charge in progress, {Role}" (no digits)) and switches to "Refill" (name "Refill, {Role}") on the tick a charge lands; above T "Rested, refill below 50%" (name "Rested, refill below 50%, {Role}"). axe `label-content-name-mismatch` = 0 for all 3 states (0 charges, Rested, enabled) in Chromium, WebKit and Firefox at 360/390/1280; the computed name has no digit in any state (CDP getFullAXTree + Playwright ariaSnapshot) and the button has no `aria-label` attribute.
  - **RF-AC9** (AC-RF8, sim). Given threshold 50%, then there are 0 dead zones per model on every seed, and worst-seed idle loss vs rc1 is ≤10%.
  - **RF-AC10** (AC list Telemetry; v1.2 decision 7). `refill_used`, `rest_toggled`, `shift_sheet_opened` and `first_worker_hired` are pinned. `manage_*` is retired. ~~`manage_used` is exported as an alias of `refill_used` for one release.~~ **No alias (D-HOE-20261030-19 G-4): wire and export are `refill_used` only** (name-only amendment; HoP signs). **v1.3 (gap 8, PM Market SS-36, sales-stopped-spec v1.3.16 `ed8a177e`):** `shift_sheet_opened {entry: nav|card|stall_chip|fatigue_chip, sheet_id, stall_id?}` (`sheet_id` per open; `stall_id` only when entry=stall_chip); `refill_used {worker, energy_pct, charges_left, rank_by_risk, by: manual|auto, sheet_id, entry_point: nav|card|stall_chip|fatigue_chip|auto, stall_id?}` and `rest_toggled {worker, on, energy_pct, sheet_id, entry_point, stall_id?}` (`sheet_id` null and `entry_point` `auto` for by:auto outside a sheet). Manage→refill conversion = `shift_sheet_opened{entry:stall_chip}` joined on `sheet_id` to `refill_used` (or `entry_point`=stall_chip alone). Lighter-refill v0.8.11 `fa074d29845befee41b828c1a3b22454457fd9e1` uses the same name (`refill` renamed, never implemented, no alias). **v1.3.1 (D-PMW-2026-10-30-10; approved D-HOE-20261030-18 + 13:16 addendum):** `refill_used` adds `refillable_n` (count), `best_worker` and `lowest_worker` (enums of code ids `market_hand|coop_hand|hawker|grader`, ties market_hand→coop_hand→hawker→grader; D-HOE-20261030-19 G-10 supersedes 'W1–W4'); `rest_toggled` is one merged event `{worker, on, energy_pct: int 0–100, state: enum, cause: manual|auto_full|manage, sheet_id, entry_point: nav|card|stall_chip|fatigue_chip|auto, stall_id?}` (rc2 L171 and the v1.3 line superseded); `session_end.shortage_s` (int, whole seconds of active, attended, unhidden time with `refillable_n` > floor(charges), sim-accumulated per tick, deterministic, session-scoped; owner Saves row 11) ships in v5. **v1.3.3 (PM Market SS-G36, PMW-confirmed 13:20 CET; HoE allowlist APPROVED, D-HOE-20261030-18 13:21 addendum; enums per PMC errata rev. 6 `83424120`):** `shift_sheet_opened.entry`, `refill_used.entry_point` and `rest_toggled.entry_point` include `fatigue_chip`, used whenever the sheet opens from the fatigue cue (popover Manage below 740 px high; sub-line tap at ≥740 px if it opens the sheet); no `stall_id`, `sheet_id` as usual. The sheet opens focused on the row heading of the named worker, or the costliest (highest `workerAtRisk`, ties W1→W4) when several are named. Fatigue-cue conversion = `shift_sheet_opened{entry:fatigue_chip}` joined on `sheet_id` to `refill_used{by:manual}` / `rest_toggled`.
  - **RF-AC11** (AC-FC16 (c)). The flag-on trace hash is pinned at build by HoE with the rc2 PR. It is AC-LR7's baseline.
  - **RF-AC12 exclusive emission** (rc2 v1.3.1 Telemetry; pause v1.3.11 §12; D-HOE-20261030-18). For every Rest/Pause toggle (manual on, manual off, `auto_full`, Manage/Refill on a resting worker), the ring holds exactly one event: with `rc2Refill` = 1 (and `pause` = 1) only `rest_toggled` (`on:true` also carries `farm`, `mood`, `unpaid`, `ability_active`, `golden_on_screen`, `frenzy_active` (HoE H-1; G-3 confirmed D-HOE-20261030-19); `on:false` also carries `paused_duration_s`); with `rc2Refill` = 0 and `pause` = 1 only `worker_paused`/`worker_resumed`; with both 0, none. Both families are in `isPinned`; no toggle is double-emitted. `session_end.paused_worker_s`/`all_paused_s` are present **only with `rc2Refill` = 0**; with `rc2Refill` = 1 they are absent and paused time = the sum of `rest_toggled{on:false}.paused_duration_s` over on:true→on:false pairs (D-HOE-20261030-19; rc2 v1.3.7, pause v1.3.13).
  - **RF-AC13 field types / no free text** (D-HOE-20261030-17/-18). A schema test over `shift_sheet_opened`, `refill_used`, `rest_toggled`, `worker_paused`, `worker_resumed` and `session_end.shortage_s` passes: `sheet_id` is a per-session monotonic integer counter (no UUID or string); `stall_id`, `entry`, `entry_point`, `by`, `state`, `cause`, `mood`, `worker`, `best_worker`, `lowest_worker` are enums; `rank_by_risk`, `charges_left`, `refillable_n`, `farm`, `paused_duration_s`, `shortage_s` are integer counts; `on`/`unpaid`/`ability_active`/`golden_on_screen`/`frenzy_active` are booleans; every worker field (`worker`, `best_worker`, `lowest_worker`) only accepts the code ids `market_hand|coop_hand|hawker|grader` (W1–W4 fails; D-HOE-20261030-19 G-10); `energy_pct` is an integer 0–100. No field holds free text; any value outside its enum or range fails.
  - **RF-AC14 isPinned + schema tests** (D-HOE-20261030-17/-18). `src/telemetry/telemetry.ts` `isPinned` returns true for `refill_used`, `rest_toggled`, `shift_sheet_opened`, `first_worker_hired`, `worker_paused` and `worker_resumed`, and the allowlist contains exactly the fields above; each has a schema test (src/telemetry/schema.ts, manifest row 11, Saves). Flags-0 RC: none of these events fire. `first_worker_hired` is gated on `flags.rc2Refill` and carries `via_nudge` = false in v5; `first_hire_nudge_shown`/`_dismissed` are not on the v5 allowlist and never fire (first-hire nudge deferred to 5.1, D-HOP-2026-10-30-36; nudge spec v0.1.1 `2786a08f`). `session_end.shortage_s` accumulates deterministically on sim ticks (same seed and inputs give the same value; 0 while hidden or offline).
  - **RF-AC15** (AC-RF4 v1.1; rc2 v1.3.2 B1; D-HOP-2026-10-30-35). Given `abilities` = 1 and `rc2Refill` = 1, each Shift-sheet row shows a Boost slot; with either flag 0 the slot is absent from the DOM. ~~A Boost started ≤10.0 s after a refill lasts 45 s ±0.5 s; one started at ≥10.1 s lasts 30 s ±0.5 s.~~ SG-3 boundary: the window is measured in sim ms from the manual refill tap to the Boost tap, inclusive: 10000 ms = combo (45 s ±0.5 s), 10001 ms = no combo (30 s ±0.5 s), and 10.05 s (10050 ms) is out. Only a manual refill (`refill_used{by:manual}`) opens it, only a Shift-sheet Boost (`ability_used{in_sheet:true}`) gets it, and `ability_used.combo` is a bool on every emit (D-HOP-2026-10-30-42). Only a manual refill (refill_used{by:manual}) opens the combo window; an auto refill never does. After an auto refill, a Boost within 10.0 s lasts 30 s ±0.5 s (rc2 v1.3.9 AC-RF4). Boost on a resting worker is `aria-disabled` and reads "Paused". Each Boost emits exactly one `ability_used{in_sheet:true}` (abilities v1.3.4 in-sheet entry) and no other event. Activate-all stays in the Workers tab (not in the sheet header). Art signs the slot in the Sat 31 Oct 12:00 CET abilities sign; if HoE or Art size it at over 1 day it is a schedule slip to HoP, not a cut.
  - **RF-AC16 Shift sheet close (build AC; rc2 v1.3.7 AC-RF9; D-HOE-20261030-19).** The sheet isn't in `src` yet, so this is a build AC, not a verify item. Given `rc2Refill` = 1, each sheet close fires exactly one `sheetCloseComplete` callback at every width (360/390/1024 docked/1280). A Boost tap closes the sheet and the call starts on that callback (start tick = callback tick). The sheet registers with the F6 overlay (UI owns it), not attended until close-complete.
  - **RF-AC17 Manage cooldown skip (build AC M-5; rc2 v1.3.7 AC-RF10; D-HOE-20261030-20 M-5).** With `rc2Refill` = 1, `src/sim/tick.ts:55` skips the Manage cooldown (`manageCdS` never gates a refill), covered by a test; with `rc2Refill` = 0 the 30 s cooldown path is byte-identical (RF-AC1).
  - Not gates: AC-RF5 is a post-launch telemetry check (v1.2). AC-RF7 (Refill all) was dropped.
- **UI check:** Given `rc2Refill` = 1, when screenshots are taken at 360/390/1280 (plus 1024 docked for RF-AC2) in Chromium, WebKit and Firefox, then they are checked against Art signed m5-refill-flows v1.0.1 43828d0b. States: Shift sheet with 2 charges, 0 charges with countdown, worker above 50%, Resting, the card with a needs-refill cue, and the nav button.
- **AC7 row:**
  - Flag-off: covered by RF-AC1 (goldens) plus AC7 hashes byte-identical to the v5 flags-0 baseline.
  - Flag-on: `rc2Refill` = 1 with `autoRefill` = 0 and `clockWage` = 0, shipped CSV. E-G4 P95 ≤180 s for optimal, seller15 and casual, seeds 1–200. The bot refills through the sheet at `refill_threshold` 50% under charge limits; this policy is Econ's to pin (D-HOP-2026-10-30-16 (4)).
  - **AC7 flag-on blocked until Econ bot-config doc sha** (Econ owns flag-on bot configs; PMW configs are input, D-HOP-2026-10-30-16 (4)).

My 12:35 "RF-AC3/RF-AC8 a11y name not testable" note is **closed** by v0.3.18 (the name contains the visible text; the axe bar is set). **QA (failure-cost v0.3.18 `af9850b5` re-review, QA-D-1030-M5QA-FC18): name and axe bar are testable; the markup/name source is a spec DEFECT (open, PMW).** v0.3.18 says the digits are "generated content in an `aria-hidden` `::after` span". `aria-hidden` can't apply to a pseudo-element, and `::after` content is part of the accessible name in Chromium, Firefox and WebKit unless its element is inside `aria-hidden="true"`. #83 ba398b6 uses a separate, text-free `<span class="nav-cd" aria-hidden="true">` with `::after{content:attr(data-cd)}`, *and* takes the name from `aria-label` ("Refill, cooling down"). This spec says "Never `aria-label`" (L55, L106), so nothing in it produces " progress, {Role}". It needs: (i) markup = DOM text "Next charge in" + a text-free `aria-hidden="true"` span with `::after{content:attr(data-cd)}` (or the CSS alt syntax `content: attr(data-cd) / ""`); (ii) one named name source for "Next charge in progress, {Role}" / "Rested, refill below 50%, {Role}" / "Refill, {Role}": `aria-label` (which lifts the never-aria-label rule for this button) or a visually hidden span. Until then QA tests the outcome, not the mechanism (B3–B5).

Given A = `tc_autorefill_at_pct` = 30 and a worker on auto at 31% with 2 charges, when it drains to 30%, then exactly 1 charge is spent, the worker is at 100%, and `refill_used{by:"auto"}` fires once. With 1 charge, charges go 1 → 0. With 0 charges there is no spend, and auto fires on the first tick with charges ≥1.
  - **AR-AC4** (AC-LR3, AC-LR4). Given a worker on auto at 31% and 10 min offline, then no auto refill happens and it returns at 31%. Given a paused or Unpaid worker at 5% with 2 charges, then there is no auto spend.
  - **AR-AC5** (AC-LR5, AC-LR15). Given two workers crossing on the same tick with 1 charge, the higher `workerAtRisk` gets it (ties: lower energy, then W1→W4). Given charges 1, A crossing 30% on tick k, and a manual Refill of B on tick k, then B is refilled, A isn't, and exactly one `refill_used` event fires.
  - **AR-AC6** (AC-LR8, AC-LR13). Given a call at 35% whose cost takes the worker to 25%, then the call starts, auto fires on the next tick, and the call isn't cancelled. Given a worker on auto at 0% with 0 charges, auto refills it on the first allowed tick with `refill_used{by:"auto", energy_pct:0}`.

  - **AB-AC3** (AC-M5A-2). Given the tab visible and no menu open, each effect lasts 30.0 s ±0.5 of focus time and then ends with `cause:"expired"`. Given an Asleep, Unpaid or (with `pause` = 1) Paused worker, then the button is `aria-disabled` with exactly "Asleep", "Unpaid" or "Paused" inside it, and a tap logs `ability_blocked`. Given a call at 20%, energy after 30 s = 20 − 30/9 ±0.1. Given Asleep or Unpaid mid-call, the effect ends within 1 tick and `readyAt` is unchanged.
  - **AB-AC6** (AC-M5A-7, AC-M5A-9). Activate-all works per AC-M5A-7. Toggling the left control between N = 1 and N = 0 moves Activate-all's rect by 0 px at 360 and 1280.

  - **AB-AC8** (AC-M5A-14). An rc1 save loads at F10+ with every call ready. SAVE_VERSION is unchanged. The previous build loads the new save without throwing.

Activate-all has no `aria-label`, and its name matches `/^Shift calls \(\d\)$/`, or exactly `Next call` / `No calls ready` when disabled (v1.3.4 D1: no digits; the time is in describedby `Next call ready in m:ss`). AC-M5A-8 fit set: call Active at every value `0:01`…`0:30` (D4: `0:00` never renders). No U+26A1, U+23F8 or U+25B6 appears in any text node. An active button reads "Active 0:ss" with its fill and 4 px strip at 100%, then 50%, then 0% (±2); they are the only active timer, with no ring on the call button (D3, AC-M5A-18). **Names (AC-M5A-23, D2):** from content only (no `aria-label`), DOM order icon · text · `aria-hidden` `data-cd` · `.sr`; call names carry the role suffix (`Rush, Market Hand`; `{Call} recharging, {Role}`; `{Call} after a break, {Role}`); no digit; **exact-string on the raw computed name, no whitespace normalisation** (Art + HoQA 13:15; NAMENORM withdrawn), so the name never matches `/ ,/` (Art F1); for every call state (card button and Shift sheet Boost slot) and Activate-all state, 3 engines × 360/390/1280: **no `aria-label` attribute on the control, and the exact computed name equals X** (X = spec §5.6 name). axe `label-content-name-mismatch` is **inapplicable** to these content-named controls (it only evaluates elements with an `aria-label`, so it passes trivially; HoQA 13:20) and is not an oracle here. Read method: CDP `getFullAXTree` in Chromium, Playwright `ariaSnapshot` in all 3 engines (O-1). Digits appear only in the `aria-hidden` `data-cd` `::after` countdown text and in describedby text, never in a name (O-3). Shift-sheet Boost: starts on `sheetCloseComplete` on focusClock, combo window (≤10.0 s) on sim time from refill tap to Boost tap, `ability_used{in_sheet:true}`; Boost slot names PROPOSED pending Art v0.2 (spec §5.6, O-7; combo-in-v5 for HoP).

- **ACs:**
  - **CW-AC1** (AC-FC7 (a)). Given `clockWage` = 0, then sim traces are byte-identical to the goldens `f90d1678…` (flag-off) and `a54c9cf9…` (M4-on) via `flagoff-golden.a01faea.test.ts` / `m4on-golden.90a9c84.test.ts` plus storage pins, and no new state, save or event fields are written.
  - **CW-AC2** (AC-FC1). Given F12, MH hired, coins ≥100,000 cents, 10 Hz, and the MH held Asleep for 120 s from c₀ (0 < c₀ ≤ 1.0 s), then it's charged exactly 120 times at c₀ … c₀ + 119 s, 5 cents each, 600 cents in total, with no ±1 tolerance. Each charge emits a `wage{state:"asleep"}`, and the MH makes 0 sales.
  - **CW-AC3** (AC-FC2). The same for the CH: exactly 60 charges in 120 s, 20 cents each, 1,200 cents in total.
  - **CW-AC4** (AC-FC3, AC-FC4). A paused MH or CH pays 0 over 120 s. Given F10, CH Asleep and coins below one interval's wage, then the CH becomes Unpaid on that interval, the card shows "Resumes at $X", and coins never go negative.
  - **CW-AC5** (AC-FC5, offline/hidden). CH Asleep offline 10 min pays 0 wage. Hidden 40 s pays the same as a focused 40 s (±1 interval). Hidden exactly 60.0 s counts as offline: 0 wage, and state reverts to the hide snapshot.
  - **CW-AC6** (AC-FC18, U1). (a) Asleep with `cdS` = 2.5 s: the first charge is on step 25, then 8 charges / 40 cents in 100 steps. (b) A Refill keeps `cdS` at 0.4 s, and the first sale is on step 4, then step 14. (c) 11 Asleep↔Working toggles over 1,200 steps give exactly 120 charges, 600 cents.
  - **CW-AC7** (AC-FC19, U2). The resume need is MH 300 / CH 600 cents. (a) With a Working MH, coins 899 leave the CH Unpaid and coins 900 resume it. (b) With an Asleep MH, coins 599 / 600. (c) On the resume step, `cdS` = 0 and 20 cents are charged in the same step. (d) The CH is back to Unpaid within 40.0 s, and coins are ≥0 at every step.
  - **CW-AC8** (AC-FC8, AC-FC9, AC-FC10, UI/a11y). Within 1 s of Asleep, `.st` reads "Asleep" (inline `icon-moon-ink.svg`, contrast ≥4.5:1) with the caption "Still paid $X/min", with no wrap at 360/390/1280 in 3 engines up to $15.00/min. The toast reads exactly "Market Hand fell asleep. Auto-selling stopped, wage still running." (or the CH string), ≤2 lines. Aria naming per AC-FC10.
  - **CW-AC9** (AC-FC11, AC-FC12). The first yawn shows the one-time tip "Sleeping workers still get paid. Refill to wake them." Welcome-back shows "Coop Hand slept 2:00 and was paid $Y"; offline time adds 0.
  - **CW-AC10** (AC-FC13, AC-FC17; D-HOP-2026-10-30-13; v0.3.19; D-HOP-2026-10-30-19 (2) as amended 12:39). At 0 charges the **Shift-sheet row's** Refill button (never the card) reads "Next charge in m:ss", rounded up and never "0:00". Above T it reads "Rested, refill below 50%" (name "Rested, refill below 50%, {Role}"). An Unpaid row never shows a countdown; its name is "Refill, {Role}" (visible "Refill" + `.sr-in` ", {Role}", `aria-disabled`; FC-N1). Econ FINDING 12:26 (SUMS `4a2252c1`): X1 reads no tc_* and hardcodes its mechanic values, so Econ has to confirm this feature is modelled from the shipped CSV. If it isn't, pacing isn't covered by X1 (the same gap as tapCoins/broody). AC7 flag-off rows re-pin to the v5 RC flags-0 hash once HoE pins it (pending, doesn't block authoring). My earlier CW-AC10 notes are **closed** by v0.3.18: the name contains the visible text, the axe bar is set, the interim width bar (D-HOP-2026-10-30-19 (3)) is numeric, and L197 is fixed. **QA (failure-cost v0.3.18 `af9850b5` re-review, QA-D-1030-M5QA-FC18): name and axe bar are testable; the markup/name source is a spec DEFECT (open, PMW).** v0.3.18 says the digits are "generated content in an `aria-hidden` `::after` span". `aria-hidden` can't apply to a pseudo-element, and `::after` content is part of the accessible name in Chromium, Firefox and WebKit unless its element is inside `aria-hidden="true"`. #83 ba398b6 uses a separate, text-free `<span class="nav-cd" aria-hidden="true">` with `::after{content:attr(data-cd)}`, *and* takes the name from `aria-label` ("Refill, cooling down"). This spec says "Never `aria-label`" (L55, L106), so nothing in it produces " progress, {Role}". It needs: (i) markup = DOM text "Next charge in" + a text-free `aria-hidden="true"` span with `::after{content:attr(data-cd)}` (or the CSS alt syntax `content: attr(data-cd) / ""`); (ii) one named name source for "Next charge in progress, {Role}" / "Rested, refill below 50%, {Role}" / "Refill, {Role}": `aria-label` (which lifts the never-aria-label rule for this button) or a visually hidden span. Until then QA tests the outcome, not the mechanism (B4–B6). **Minor (record):** AC-FC17 (b) still carries the old "Refill in" figures (exactly 208 ±0.5 px; natural ≤204 px / ≥4 px headroom) and the v0.3.18 interim 208px bar. Art's floor is now 253px (inner ≥ 201px), so PMW should replace them with the 253px bar; QA applies 253px (B7).

- **ACs:**
  - **SST-AC1** (SS-21 gate 1, SS-23). Given `salesStopped` = 0, when seeds 1–200 run, then the sim/economy traces, the farm-screen DOM at 360 and 1280, and the telemetry match the patch1 golden, with 0 `sales_stall` and 0 `fatigue_cue_*` events.
  - **SST-AC2** (SS-1). Given F17, MH energy 0, CH Working, basket ≥ 100 and a full queue, then `Market Hand asleep · Refill` shows at 8.0 ± 0.1 s of focus time with no sale, and is absent at 7.9 s.
  - **SST-AC3** (SS-2, SS-3). Given the SS-AC2 chip with `manageCdS` = 0, a tap refills MH and the chip hides ≤ 100 ms with a 150 ms crossfade to the goal bubble (no empty frame). With `manageCdS` = 20 the label reads `… · Sell all` with no badge, and a tap does exactly one Sell all.
  - **SST-AC4** (SS-4, SS-5, SS-6, SS-30). Each cause shows its §4.4 label and runs its action:
    - `mh_unpaid` → Sell all;
    - `no_mh` at 20 s (never at F0–2 or before onboarding ends);
    - `ch_asleep` / `ch_unpaid` → Refill / Collect;
    - `mh_paused` at 20 s of zero sales, Sell all only, never resumes. Runs on the final v5 RC with `pause` = 1 (pause also ships ON in v5, D-CEO-20261030-09/-10 (`decisions.md` L103–117)).
  - **SST-AC5** (SS-7). Across a 600 s F10–F20 trace with all workers Working or Tired, the chip shows 0 times.
  - **SST-AC6** (SS-8, SS-9, SS-34). Each of C1–C7 hides the chip ≤ 100 ms. It reshows no earlier than max(the full hold, 5 s after the hide). A wake then re-sleep within 5 s shows no chip flash.
  - **SST-AC7** (SS-12, SS-25). With MH and CH both asleep, the chip names MH and the farm banner names CH. The banner text is verbatim rc2 §6, and the role drops only by `bannerFit`.
  - **SST-AC8** (SS-15, SS-28; patch-lane regression; **blocked: patch1 not built**). With MH Asleep or Unpaid, live `incomePerSec` = −wages and the HUD is red. `secondsToAffordSteady` = null within 1 s.
  - **SST-AC9** (SS-19). Exactly one resolved per shown (`stall_id`). `time_to_resolve_s` = the focus duration ± 0.1 s. `session_s` is on the focus clock. Pinned pairs are deduped by (role, cause).
  - **SST-AC10** (SS-20, SS-17, SS-18). The MH banner and the chip aria say "sales stopped"; CH says "collecting stopped". All 9 labels fit on one line in Fredoka 700 (worst case 242 px). The accessible name is the visible text, and there's one polite announcement.
  - **SST-AC13** (SS-36, D-HOP-2026-10-30-20 (1); no-swap CONFIRMED by HoP and Art; label **Art to sign**). Given `rc2Refill` = 1 and MH (or CH) Asleep:
    - the chip reads `<Role> asleep · Manage`, with no cooldown swap;
    - a tap opens the Shift sheet with the asleep worker's row heading scrolled into view and focused (as CW B10), with no refill spent;
    - with `rc2Refill` = 1 the chip-Refill C4 exception is N/A (SS-12 (d)); a sheet Refill is an ordinary C4 (`refill_elsewhere`);
    - `sales_stall` resolved has `resolved_by: chip_manage`, `chip_tapped: true`, emitted when the sheet opens (Option A, D-HOP-2026-10-30-20 addendum 2). It's a C6 hide, not a clear: timers are frozen;
    - Manage→Refill conversion (spec §8, v1.3.22) = chip_manage (`stall_id` S) → `shift_sheet_opened{entry: stall_chip, stall_id: S}` (`sheet_id` K) → ≥ 1 `refill_used{by: manual, sheet_id: K}` (rc2 v1.3 `2fdd84a2` L204–205). The 60 s fallback is retired (HoP). The fields need HoE allowlist approval (**pending**);
    - a Refill in that sheet logs `refill_elsewhere`, never `chip_refill` or `chip_manage`;
    - closing the sheet with the stall still holding reshows the chip as a new `stall_id` with `prev_stall_id`;
    - the worst-case label `Market Hand asleep · Manage` (about 256 px) fits one line in the 320 px slot at 360 in all 3 engines (SS-17).
  Given `rc2Refill` = 0, the label and action are as in SST-AC2/3 (242 px worst case).
  - **SST-AC11** (SS-35). A chip tap never reaches `#scene` (no Collect, credited tap or streak step). A second tap within 500 ms or during the crossfade does nothing.
  - **SST-AC12** (SS-13, SS-11). With a sheet open or the tab hidden < 60 s, the chip is hidden and the timer frozen. A gap ≥ 60 s or a welcome-back resets the timer, and the chip reshows only after the full hold.

### Card #11 — fatigueCue (`m4.flags.fatigueCue`, paired with salesStopped)
- **Errata note, 2026-10-30 14:13 CET (citation only, no AC change):** spec re-pinned to v1.3.27 `89dadb43` (= `7148a34d` + errata notes 1–2).

The flag is defined in spec §6 and SS-23. **SS-23 is an AC change** (amended in v1.3.11 with the fatigueCue = 0 clause = FC-AC1; D-HOP-2026-10-29-05/-06, -30-16). **Slot clause: SS-23f** (v1.3.13, AC amendment, Art ruling 12:39). The Rule 2 HUD chip renders in the `#rate` sub-line slot, not in `#goal`. That slot exists only when fatigueCue = 1. The escalation is cleared.
- **Art spec sha1:** **SIGNED.** Fatigue UX `art-ux/m4.2-fatigue-visibility-ux.md` **v1.3.1 re-sign** `ffb13c192ab39427aa5a519c62f8321d9bbaff35` (`art-ux/decisions.md` L560, 13:58: copy errata only, the §4 fall-asleep toast for Hawker/Egg Grader; chip, sub-line and FC-SV ACs unchanged; replaces `b23ae9e7`). Before that, **v1.3.1** `b23ae9e7b9bdbd97ee5d42fb3dd3c302596c771a` (`art-ux/decisions.md` 13:40 L541, re-sign for FC-SV14 only; first signed `b72e8ba5` 13:36 L535; adds FC-SV11–16, L53–95; evidence `cb5ca050` / SUMS `ffa115bb`) over **v1.3** `115891d4db2f310b2c9f5f305fffcbc6ba29ab63` (`art-ux/decisions.md` 13:06 L514, file `95f31259…`; replaces v1.2 `3e4dbc9f`, signed 12:44, and `df512912…` / `60e9a144…`). Its Art ACs are FC-SV1–FC-SV10 (L34–50). Evidence: `art-ux/reviews/fc-ac9-golden-spots-360.md` `34e86e2288add698b656dbb5b1623a134c919272` (SHA256SUMS `051eca86…`), rc/v5 e77fc5c, 3 engines. The 390 rect is `reviews/m4.2-fatigue-390-rect.md` `ea53de1c…` (L482). Icons: `icon-warning.svg` `31afd1b3…`, `ico_fatigue_zzz.svg` `d4709f93…`, `ico_fatigue_sweat.svg` `cbc1665d…` (L481). Rects come from `m5-layout-rects-960.md` `54a59d49…` and v1.3 §1 L15–25. Spec v1.3.13 is signed (L480).
- **ACs:**
  - **FC-AC1** (D-HOP-2026-10-29-05, pairing). Given `salesStopped` = 1 and `fatigueCue` = 0, then the stall chip and Rules 3–4 behave per SST-AC1–12, and there is no Rule 2 HUD chip and no `fatigue_cue_shown{surface:hud_chip}`. Given `salesStopped` = 0, then `fatigueCue` has no effect.
  - **FC-AC2** (SS-24). Given share ≥ 0.15, the chip shows within 6 s, and not at 0.14. Once shown it stays at 0.13 and clears within 3 s at 0.11. X = Σ `workerAtRisk` ± 1%. The text is [icon-warning] `Tired −$X/s` below 400 px and `Tired workers −$X/s` at ≥ 400 px. **Copy at innerHeight ≤ 739 (PMW CONFIRMED, D-PMW-2026-10-30-10, 13:13):** visible copy is icon-only. The full `Tired −$X/s` goes in the popover, and the `.sr` name keeps the full sentence, with FC-AC2 copy as `aria-describedby`. Copy cases: 360×640 shows the icon + popover; 390×844 shows the text pill.
  - **FC-AC3** (SS-31). A Paused worker of any role isn't counted in `fatigueLoss`, and `n_paused` counts it.
  - **FC-AC4** (SS-32). With share 0.14 and the chip hidden, starting or ending Rush, Shout, Frenzy or a streak leaves the chip state unchanged.
  - **FC-AC5** (SS-12). The chip is hidden while the stall chip shows. It returns ≤ 1 s after a C2, C3 or C6 clear if its Rule 2 state is still "shown". After a C4 Refill with share < 0.12, it doesn't return.
  - **FC-AC6** (SS-15 share guard). With a denominator ≤ 0, share = 1 if `fatigueLoss` > 0, else 0. Never NaN or Infinity.
  - **FC-AC7** (SS-27 pattern). AC7 bot trace hashes on seeds 1–200 are byte-identical with `fatigueCue` on vs off (with `salesStopped` = 1).
  - **FC-AC8 = SS-23f** (slot; Art ruling 12:39). Given `fatigueCue` = 0 (any `salesStopped`), then the `#rate` sub-line slot is absent and the `#rate`/HUD DOM and rects equal the patch1 golden for that row. Given `fatigueCue` = 1 (`salesStopped` = 1), then the slot is reserved at all times, empty when no chip shows, with 0 px layout shift when the chip shows or hides.
  - **FC-AC9** (= spec **SS-37**, v1.3.21; D-HOP-2026-10-30-20 (2), D-HOP-2026-10-30-24; Art fatigue-ux v1.3 `115891d4` L13–14). For each state (pill hidden / pill shown / Broody Y5), the valid golden-egg spot count with `fatigueCue` = 1 equals flag-off (**8/7/4**), and the Broody-eligible slots equal flag-off.
    - **(i) Viewport height ≤ 739 (icon chip built):** at 360×640, 375×667 and 360×739, with the popover closed **and** open.
    - **(ii) Height ≥ 740 (sub-line):** at 360×740, 360×780 and 390×844. **Measured 8/7/4 at all six viewports in 3 engines** (Art 13:06). The measured floor is 672 px; 740 is kept as margin.
    - **Why:** the sub-line at 360×640 measured **5/5/3** vs 8/7/4 (S4/S8 14 px from `#meter`, S5 15.5 px from Full!), so it **failed** and the short-viewport icon chip was introduced. Evidence: `art-ux/reviews/fc-ac9-golden-spots-360.md` `34e86e2288add698b656dbb5b1623a134c919272` (SHA256SUMS `051eca86…`) §1–3; Art rulings 12:58 (decisions L499) and 13:06 (L514).
    - **Fixes:** (a) not needed (no effect), (b) as a full-text inline pill superseded by Art's icon-only chip, (c) not needed.
    - **Related ACs (spec → Art):**

  - **TEL-AC1 envelope.** Given any exported event, then it has `type` (string, on the allowlist), `ts` (number, ms), `sid` (6-char base36) and `fgMs` (number ≥ 0, non-decreasing within a `sid`). It has no `sessionMs` (telemetry-spec L17–20, T-AC1 L143).
  - **TEL-AC2 session_start.** Given a fresh load, then exactly one `session_start` fires before any other event of that `sid`, with:
    - `install_id`, `build`, `session_id`, `session_index`, `reason` ∈ {load, resume}, `farm_level`, `collection_level`, `has_coop_hand` (bool), `reduced_motion` (bool);
    - `viewport_w` ∈ {`<375`, `375–767`, `≥768`} (L27);
    - **new `viewport_h` ∈ {`'<740'`, `'740-899'`, `'>=900'`}** (HoE-approved, D-HOE-20261030-18 L376; errata V-4): a bucket of `innerHeight` (CSS px) at session_start. It is never raw and not re-sent on resize (sales-stopped v1.3.27 `7148a34d` §8 L301–305; D-HOP-2026-10-30-28 (1)).
  - **TEL-AC3 viewport_h boundary (HoE).**
    - `innerHeight` = 739 → `viewport_h = '<740'`, and the fatigue cue renders as the icon chip.
    - 740 → `'740-899'`, and the sub-line.
    - 899 → `'740-899'`; 900 → `'>=900'`.
    - The 740 edge comes from the single constant shared with the CSS breakpoint (sales-stopped v1.3.27 `7148a34d` §4.8 L150–152, SS-38 (b)(e)).
  - **TEL-AC4 sales_stall schema and pair invariants** (sales-stopped v1.3.27 `7148a34d` §8 L276–281):
    - `phase` ∈ {shown, resolved}, plus `stall_id`, optional `prev_stall_id`.
    - `cause` ∈ {mh_asleep, mh_paused, mh_unpaid, no_mh, ch_asleep, ch_unpaid, other}.
    - `mh_state` / `ch_state` ∈ {working, tired, asleep, unpaid, paused, none}.
    - Also `farm_level`, `queue_len`, `sellable_eggs`, `coop_eggs`, `focus_s_since_last_sale`, `session_s` (focus clock).
    - Resolved adds `resolved_by` ∈ {chip_refill, chip_sell_all, chip_collect, **chip_manage**, refill_elsewhere, sell_elsewhere, collect_elsewhere, auto_resume, queue_empty, basket_empty, paused, focus_lost, cause_changed, none}, `time_to_resolve_s` (focus clock, 1 dp) and `chip_tapped` (bool).
    - Invariants:
      - Exactly one resolved per shown (by `stall_id`).
      - C6 closes the pair with `focus_lost`, except a chip tap opening the Shift sheet, which closes it with `chip_manage`.
      - A reshow is a new `stall_id` with `prev_stall_id`.
      - Pinned pairs are deduped by (role, cause) within an episode.
      - Pinned.
    - **Field types (PM Market, sales-stopped v1.3.27 `7148a34d` §8 L289–300; **adopted in errata rev 4, carried in rev 6 `83424120`** V-2 #1, L55, signed off L51):**
      - `stall_id` / `prev_stall_id`: int, min 1, a per-session monotonic counter, never saved (restarts each session). Pairs are keyed by (`sid`, `stall_id`). **HoE D-HOE-20261030-24** (13:48, `engineering/decisions.md` L439–440; errata rev 7 N-1) supersedes the old 'enum' wording at L380. Saves already has it in schema.ts.
      - `farm_level`, `queue_len`: int ≥ 0. `sellable_eggs` / `coop_eggs`: int ≥ 0, `Math.floor` of the fractional `basket` / `coop`.
      - `focus_s_since_last_sale`, `session_s`, `time_to_resolve_s`: float s ≥ 0, 1 dp. `chip_tapped`: bool.
      - Enums as listed. Optional fields are absent, never null.
  - **TEL-AC5 fatigue_cue_*.**
    - `fatigue_cue_shown {surface ∈ {hud_chip, banner, bubble}, loss_per_s, loss_share, n_tired, n_asleep, n_paused, suppressed_by_stall: bool}` and `fatigue_cue_tapped {surface, loss_per_s}` (sales-stopped v1.3.27 `7148a34d` §8 L282).
    - The ≤ 739 icon chip logs `surface: hud_chip`. There's **no** `hud_icon` (D-HOP-2026-10-30-24).
    - **Firing (F-4c ruling 13:25; G37 ruled 13:33; sales-stopped v1.3.27 `7148a34d` (FROZEN for QA) §8 L283–287 (firing rule L283; episode end L285 = Rule 2 hysteresis, share < 0.12 held 2 s; SS-G39 L286; mid-episode stall L287), SS-47–SS-49; HoQA F-4 (b)(c), `qa/m5/review-telemetry-errata-v5-rev2.md` `c915d911`; errata rev 6 `83424120` V-2c L102–103):**
      - `fatigue_cue_shown` fires **once per cue episode**, at the moment the cue becomes eligible (Rule 2 show condition met). `suppressed_by_stall` = true if the stall chip is up at that moment (cue hidden), else false.
      - If the stall chip appears mid-episode (SS-G35), the popover closes and focus moves to the stall chip. **No event** is logged, and the episode continues. `fatigue_cue_shown` doesn't re-fire when the stall clears and the cue reappears.
      - The episode ends when the cue condition clears under the Rule 2 hysteresis: share < `tc_fatigue_cue_clear_share` 0.12 held `tc_fatigue_cue_clear_hold_s` 2 s. The next eligibility is a new episode.
      - `fatigue_cue_tapped` fires **once per chip tap, including a tap that closes the popover** (SS-G37 **RULED**; open + close = 2 events; no new field). That's one event for the sub-line tap at ≥ 740 and one for each icon-chip tap at ≤ 739. Esc, an outside tap, a resize and cue resolution emit none. The popover open/close emits nothing more. The popover's Manage emits only `shift_sheet_opened{entry: fatigue_chip}`. **Adopted in errata rev 4 `9e02ac3a` V-2c L102.**
      - `fatigue_cue_*` has **no shown/resolved pair** and no resolved event.
    - Both events are pinned (errata V-3).
    - **Field types (PM Market, sales-stopped v1.3.27 `7148a34d` §8 L289–300; **adopted in errata rev 4, carried in rev 6 `83424120`** V-2 #2–3, L56–57):**
      - `loss_per_s`: int cents/s ≥ 0 = `Math.round(fatigueLoss × 100)`. The code computes float dollars/s (`src/econ/income.ts` L72–79), and this is the same rounding as `salesStopped()` L82.
      - `loss_share`: float in [0, 1], 3 dp, with the §4.8 share guard.
      - `n_tired` / `n_asleep` / `n_paused`: int ≥ 0, each ≤ 4, sum ≤ 4 (4 `WorkerId` roles, `src/sim/workers.ts` L4).
      - `surface`: enum. `suppressed_by_stall`: bool. No nulls.
  - **TEL-AC6 rc2 events and field types** (rc2 v1.3.3 L204–214; HoE D-HOE-20261030-18 L376, L390):
    - `shift_sheet_opened {entry ∈ {nav, card, stall_chip, fatigue_chip}, sheet_id, stall_id?}`. `stall_id` is present iff entry = `stall_chip`.
    - `refill_used {worker, energy_pct, charges_left, rank_by_risk, by ∈ {manual, auto}, sheet_id|null, entry_point ∈ {nav, card, stall_chip, fatigue_chip, auto}, stall_id?, refillable_n, best_worker, lowest_worker}`.
      - `sheet_id` is null only when `by` = auto outside a sheet.
      - `entry_point` equals the opening `shift_sheet_opened.entry`, or `auto`.
      - The RF5 fields come from rc2 L208 and HoE's 13:16 addendum.
    - **`rest_toggled` (one merged event; D-PMW-2026-10-30-10; rc2 v1.3.6 L215):** `{worker, on: bool, energy_pct: int 0–100, state: enum (worker state at toggle), cause ∈ {manual, auto_full, manage}, sheet_id, entry_point ∈ {nav, card, stall_chip, fatigue_chip, auto}, stall_id?}`. `cause: manage` = a Refill on a resting worker ends Rest (pause L148).
      - With `rc2Refill` = 1 it also carries the Pause-only fields: on `on: true`, `farm`, `mood` (enum), `unpaid` (bool), `ability_active` (bool), **`golden_on_screen` (bool) and `frenzy_active` (bool)** (HoE H-1 fields, sampled from the pre-pause state; rc2 v1.3.8, pause v1.3.14 §12, errata rev 6 `83424120` V-2 #6 L60; D-HOE-20261030-19 G-3); on `on: false`, `paused_duration_s`.
    - **`fatigue_chip`** is the entry/entry_point value when the Shift sheet opens from the fatigue cue (popover Manage at ≤ 739; rc2 v1.3.3 L214, PMW-confirmed 13:20; SS-G36).
      - **HoE APPROVED** (D-HOE-20261030-18 13:21 addendum, `engineering/decisions.md` `ba52827b` L390): it's an enum, not free text, carries no `stall_id`, and uses `sheet_id` as usual. Saves adds it to schema.ts.
      - The allowlist line is in errata rev 6 `83424120` V-2c, L103 (also in V-2 #4 L58 and #5 L59).
    - **Field types (D-HOE-20261030-18 L376; rc2 L211):**
      - `sheet_id` is a **per-session monotonic int counter** (no UUID or string), unique per open within a `sid`.
      - Enums: `entry`, `entry_point`, `by`, `state`, `cause`, `mood`, `worker`, `best_worker`, `lowest_worker`. **Worker wire values are the code ids `market_hand|coop_hand|hawker|grader`** (`src/sim/workers.ts:4`; **D-HOE-20261030-19 G-10**). W1–W4 is spec shorthand only; this supersedes "enum W1–W4" for `best_worker` / `lowest_worker` in D-HOE-18.
      - Integer counts: `stall_id` / `prev_stall_id` (min 1, per-session counter, never saved; D-HOE-20261030-24), `rank_by_risk`, `charges_left`, `refillable_n`, `farm`, `paused_duration_s`, `shortage_s`.
      - Booleans: `on`, `unpaid`, `ability_active`, `in_sheet`, `combo` (the last two on `ability_used`, required; rev 6).
      - `energy_pct` is an int 0–100.
      - No free text. Any value outside its type, enum or range fails.
    - `session_end.shortage_s` (int, whole seconds; rc2 L209; HoE 13:16 addendum; owner Saves, row 11). Saves confirmed it at 13:37: it's in schema.ts, with accumulation in the telemetry subset PR.
    - No event named `refill` exists (lighter-refill v0.8.10 L62). The refill event is **`refill_used`** on the wire and in exports. There is no `manage_used` event and no alias (closed schema; **D-HOP-2026-10-30-41 (2)**, `product/decisions.md` L1880; D-HOE-20261030-19 G-4; errata rev 6 `83424120` L74). **HoP SIGNED** this name-only amendment (**D-HOP-2026-10-30-43**, `product/decisions.md` L1951).
    - **`refill_used.by` (`manual|auto`) is required on every emit** (errata rev 6 `83424120` L59, L64; D-HOP-2026-10-30-42). An event without `by` fails the schema test.
  - **TEL-AC7 conversion join (sales-stopped v1.3.27 `7148a34d` §8 L307–326).**
    - Given a `sales_stall{resolved, resolved_by: chip_manage, stall_id: S}`, then exactly one `shift_sheet_opened{entry: stall_chip, stall_id: S}` exists in the same `sid` at `ts` ≥ the resolved `ts`.
    - Every `refill_used{sheet_id: K}` that follows it has `entry_point: stall_chip` and `stall_id: S`.
    - Fatigue-cue conversion is `shift_sheet_opened{entry: fatigue_chip, sheet_id: K}` → `refill_used{sheet_id: K, entry_point: fatigue_chip}`, with no `stall_id` (rc2 L214).
  - **TEL-AC8 other feature events, schemas verbatim from their specs:**
    - `collectible_*` / `frenzy_*` / `streak_*` / `tap` and `session_end` extensions (golden §8, streak §8/§8.1, broody §8: `skin:"broody_hen"`, `slot_id` Y0–Y11);
    - `abilities_unlocked`, `ability_used` (**`in_sheet` bool, required on every emit**; abilities v1.3.9 `133adb21` L437; **`combo` bool, required on every emit** (errata rev 6 `83424120` L64; D-HOP-2026-10-30-42): `true` only when this Boost got the 45 s combo duration. Only `refill_used{by:"manual"}` opens the 10 s window (sim ms, ≤ 10000 inclusive), and `by:"auto"` never does. Only `in_sheet:true` Boosts qualify; a card-button call is always `combo:false`. No window-remaining field), `ability_blocked` (≤ 1 per worker per reason per 1.0 s, with `count`; **cap: at most 50 per session, then one `ability_blocked{capped:true}` and no more that session, reset on session rotation**, errata rev 6 `83424120` V-3 L117–119: marker `{type:"ability_blocked", capped:true, events:50}`, plus `session_end.ability_blocked_taps` int ≥ 0 counting every blocked tap including post-cap ones (D-HOE-20261030-23 (a)(b)); **HoE-approved, D-HOE-20261030-19 G-1**), `ability_ended` (cause enum final) (abilities §9);
    - `worker_paused` / `worker_resumed` (pause §12) and **`session_end.paused_worker_s` / `all_paused_s` are emitted only with `rc2Refill` = 0** (and `pause` = 1; TEL-AC14). With `rc2Refill` = 1 they are **absent**, and paused time = Σ `rest_toggled{on:false}.paused_duration_s` (**D-HOE-20261030-19 G-3**, L392; pause v1.3.13+ §12 L298; RF-AC12). This supersedes the earlier "both builds" reading (PMW 13:25, errata rev 3). It matches **errata rev 6 `83424120` L139** (unchanged since rev 4) and D-HOP-2026-10-30-41's 13:36 amendment.
    - **`session_end.ability_blocked_taps`** (int count, min 0; every blocked tap in the session, including those after the 50 cap; no free text; flag `abilities`; follows the `session_end` pin status). Source: **D-HOE-20261030-23 (b)**, `engineering/decisions.md` L435; errata rev 6 L119. Owner Saves, in schema.ts (likely Fri 21:00 CET, high Sat 03:00). Runnable after the subset.
    - `first_worker_hired {worker, farm_level, t_since_unlock_s, via_nudge: bool}` (nudge `1c95fc8a` §4 L15). **`via_nudge` is always `false` in v5** (D-HOP-2026-10-30-36). It is gated on `m4.flags.rc2Refill` and pinned;
    - **`worker_summary.wage_<worker>_asleep_cents`** (int cents, dynamic key, `clockWage` = 1 only; HoE FC-G11, `engineering/decisions.md` L144; failure-cost AC-FC15 L138), `session_end.wage_paid_asleep_cents` / `asleep_s`, `tip_shown{id:"clock_wage"}` (failure-cost §6). `wage.state` is **never exported**: `wage` isn't an event (telemetry-spec L54), and `wage.state` is tested on the SimEvent sink (AC-FC15). This re-points the card per HoQA F-5 (`qa/m5/review-telemetry-errata-v5-rev2.md` `c915d911`).
      - Checked on disk at 13:42: the field is in **errata rev 6 `83424120`** V-2b L88 (checked 13:47) (int cents, `clockWage` = 1 only, `<worker>` = the code worker id). Saves adds it to schema.ts as an int count, min 0 (D-HOE-20261030-22), emitted with the subset. **Build only:** the code snapshot (`src/game.ts:116`) still sums only `wage_${e.worker}_cents`;
    - `autorefill_toggled {worker, on, farm_level}`, `session_end.refills_manual` / `refills_auto` / `charge_starved_s` (lighter §6);
    - `hatch_started`, `breed_revealed`, `breed_slotted`, `flock_complete`, `offline_grant.away_bp`, and the breeds `session_end` fields incl. `present_active_s` / `present_lost_count` (breeds §8);
    - `upgrade.zero_gain` and `zero_gain_confirm` (zero-gain §11). `zero_gain_confirm` never fires while `m4.zeroGain.confirm` = 0. Keys are `m4.*` (errata V-5; D-HOE-20261030-18; `src/config/flags.ts` :4, :10, :18-19).
    - Any field or enum value not in the source spec fails.
    - *Note:* the first-hire nudge events `first_hire_nudge_shown` / `_dismissed` are out of v5 (D-HOP-2026-10-30-36). They are not on the v5 allowlist (errata "Not added").
  - **TEL-AC9 firing rules.**
    - `session_start` and `session_end` fire once per `sid`. A hidden gap ≥ 60 s rotates the session (T-AC2, T-AC13).
    - `tap` is one event per `tap_batch_ms` window (T-AC3).
    - `coop_full` fires once per transition and is capped at 100 (T-AC10).
    - `ability_blocked` coalesces as in TEL-AC8.
    - Once-per-save events fire only once and never re-emit on reload: `abilities_unlocked`, `flock_complete`, `tip_shown{clock_wage}`.
    - `sales_stall` / `fatigue_cue_*` follow TEL-AC4/5.
    - No popover, resize or variant swap emits an event (SS-38 (c), SS-40 (c)).
  - **TEL-AC10 flag-off = 0 new events.** Given the flags-0 RC config (ship-set L32), when seeds 1–200 run plus one scripted UI session, then:
    - zero events of any M5-only type are emitted (`sales_stall`, `fatigue_cue_*`, `shift_sheet_opened`, `refill_used`, `rest_toggled`, `first_worker_hired`, `autorefill_toggled`, `ability_*`, `abilities_unlocked`, `worker_paused` / `worker_resumed`, `hatch_started`, `breed_*`, `flock_complete`, `zero_gain_confirm`, `tip_shown{clock_wage}`, `collectible_*` / `frenzy_*` / `streak_*`, and the bestBuyV2-gated Best-buy rows `best_buy_shown`, `best_buy_followed`, `zero_gain_purchase`, `dead_zone` (errata rev 15 `5e12a591` V-2 #19–22)); `best_buy_suppressed_zero_gain` is **not** in this list: it isn't gated by bestBuyV2 (it needs `m4.enabled` and the stopgap #5c build, which is not built in v5) and is graded separately by the SG-8 must-be-0 check below (errata rev 15 `5e12a591` #23 L82; D-HOE-20261030-33);
    - no flag-gated field appears (e.g. `worker_summary.wage_<worker>_asleep_cents`, `upgrade.zero_gain`, `session_end` breeds / refill / wage fields, `session_end.shortage_s`, `session_end.ability_blocked_taps` with abilities = 0, `ability_used.combo` with abilities = 0);
    - the core envelope and `session_start.viewport_w` / `viewport_h` still emit (telemetry is always-on).
    - Per feature: with only that feature's flag set to 0, none of its events or fields appear. With **`rc2Refill` = 0**, `shift_sheet_opened`, `refill_used`, `rest_toggled` and **`first_worker_hired`** are not emitted (D-HOP-2026-10-30-36; errata rev 2 G-5). With **`bestBuyV2` = 0** (all else at ship values, ship-set `product/v5-ship-set.md` L19), `best_buy_shown`, `best_buy_followed`, `zero_gain_purchase` and `dead_zone` are not emitted, and no BBv2-only field appears (incl. `session_end.zero_gain_purchase_dropped`) (errata rev 15 `5e12a591` V-2, V-2b). `best_buy_suppressed_zero_gain` is not covered by this rule; it is graded by the SG-8 must-be-0 check below (errata rev 15 #23, not built in v5).
    - **No-boost check:** in every config, `boost_used` is never emitted (TEL-AC15).
    - **No-nudge check:** in every config, 0 `first_hire_nudge_*` events are emitted.
    - **SG-8 must-be-0 check (GRADED at the RC, not a merge gate; HoQA QA-D-1109-TELAC-FILL):** in every v5 config (incl. flags-0: seeds 1–200 plus the scripted UI session), **0** `best_buy_suppressed_zero_gain` events. The stopgap #5c emitter is not built in v5 and any emission is a defect (errata rev 15 `5e12a591` #23 L82: "not built in v5 (D-HOE-20261030-33); any emission is a defect"; eng. decisions L522). It is outside the bestBuyV2 flag-off rule above (gated on `m4.enabled` only, bb-stopgap v1.2.6 `fdc1c199` SG-7 L27). Ring event (errata L159), so it does not gate merge; any emission at the RC is a **MAJOR** bug. For v5 this supersedes the SG-8-only grading test of QA-D-1030-TELAC-AMD (`qa/decisions.md` L765).
  - **TEL-AC11 pinned/allowlist consistency.**
    - Every emitted type is in `src/telemetry/schema.ts` (T-AC9 L151) **and** on the telemetry-spec §3.1 allowlist (L46–56) plus errata V-2. An export with any unknown type or field fails.
    - The pinned set in code (`isPinned`) equals the union of:
      - §3.2 L60–64;
      - **18 pinned of the 23 new v5 types** (errata rev 15 `5e12a591` V-2 #1–23, V-3; the 16 listed here keep the pin status per the HoQA G-6 ruling, `qa/m5/review-telemetry-errata-v5-rev2.md` `c915d911` L141–145): `sales_stall`, `fatigue_cue_shown`, `fatigue_cue_tapped`, `shift_sheet_opened`, `refill_used`, `rest_toggled`, `first_worker_hired`, `autorefill_toggled` (HoQA: PINNED, confirmed), `abilities_unlocked`, `ability_used`, `ability_blocked` (HoQA: PINNED with a per-session cap, proposed at 50 plus one `ability_blocked{capped:true}`; PMW + HoE add the cap, F-1), `ability_ended` (HoQA: PINNED), `worker_resumed`, `tip_shown{id:"clock_wage"}`, `hatch_started`, `flock_complete`, **plus `zero_gain_purchase` (capped 50/session) and `dead_zone`** (Best buy, gated on bestBuyV2; errata rev 15 L80–81);
      - **5 ring of the 23:** `breed_revealed` and `breed_slotted` (breeds §8 L695–696, breeds 3.8.9 `ea922eb8`; erratum per D-HOP-2026-11-10-07), plus `best_buy_shown`, `best_buy_followed` and `best_buy_suppressed_zero_gain` (Best buy; `best_buy_suppressed_zero_gain` on `m4.enabled`, not built in v5; errata rev 15 L78, L79, L82);
      - plus 3 already-allowlisted types that become pinned: `worker_paused` (pause §12), and **`milestone_reached` / `balanced_tier`** (pinned per D-HOP-2026-10-29-07, `product/decisions.md` L1351; errata V-7).
      - Total `isPinned` additions = **21** (18 + 3), event-based `isPinned(e: TEvent)` (D-HOE-19 F-10; errata rev 15 L149–158). Saves re-sizes the 200 KB pinned budget for 21 entries with the worst-case-session test. **The test uses `dead_zone`'s worst-case rate from errata rev 15 `5e12a591`: ≤ 1 event per closed stretch of ≥ 30 sim s (emitted at the stretch end, plus at most one `open:true` flush on unload/background if `s` ≥ 30), only while 10 ≤ farm < 30, i.e. ≤ 120 per sim hour (1 per 30 sim s), ≈ 60 B each; the test asserts ≤ 120 `dead_zone` per 3600 sim s, including accelerated runs** (V-2d, V-3 L159; QA-D-1030-TELAC-AMD; QA-D-1109-TELAC-FILL A-1). It also covers `zero_gain_purchase` at its worst case: ≤ 1 per purchase, capped at 50 per session (buy-spam fixture, G-8; errata L80, L159, L190).
      - **errata rev 15 `5e12a591` matches:** 18 pinned / 5 ring, 21 `isPinned` additions. `ability_ended` is pinned, and `ability_blocked` is pinned with the cap of 50 + one `{capped:true}` marker per session, with totals still in `count` (**HoE-approved, D-HOE-20261030-19 G-1**). The marker key name and any post-cap total are errata G-6/R-4 (PMW + HoE). The 200 KB pinned cap stays, and Saves sizes it with a worst-case-session test in schema.ts. QA asserts the 50/51 boundary. `isPinned(e: TEvent)` is event-based for `tip_shown{clock_wage}` (D-HOE-19 F-10; Saves, in the schema.ts PR).
    - `boost_used` is **not allowlisted** and not pinned (errata "Not added"; D-HOE-20261030-18). `first_hire_nudge_*` are not allowlisted in v5 (D-HOP-2026-10-30-36).
    - Pinned store ≤ 200 KB after compaction (T-AC14), and `session_summary.start` carries `viewport_h`.
  - **TEL-AC12 no PII** (L139, T-AC9 L151).
    - No field holds a name, email, IP, UA, precise geo or free text.
    - Ids (`sid`, `stall_id`, `sheet_id`, `collectible_id`) are random or counters, never device-derived; `sheet_id` is a per-session int counter.
    - Both viewports are bucketed only.
  - **TEL-AC13 balance CSV columns (sales-stopped v1.3.27 `7148a34d` §6 L222–235).** economy.csv and the synced balance.json carry exactly the 9 `tc_*` columns:
    - `tc_stall_hold_s` 8, `tc_stall_hold_nomh_s` 20, `tc_stall_hold_paused_s` 20;
    - `tc_stall_rearm_s` 5, `tc_stall_tap_guard_ms` 500;
    - `tc_fatigue_cue_min_share` 0.15, `tc_fatigue_cue_hold_s` 5, `tc_fatigue_cue_clear_share` 0.12, `tc_fatigue_cue_clear_hold_s` 2.
    - Values are within range, `clear_share` < `min_share`, and the `tc_stall_hold_s` floor follows SS-22. `sync-economy --check` passes.
  - **TEL-AC14 one rest/pause family per build (D-PMW-2026-10-30-10; D-HOE-20261030-18).** For each pause/resume toggle, exactly one event is emitted, never both families:
    - with `pause` = 1 and `rc2Refill` = 1: only `rest_toggled` (pause → `on: true`, resume → `on: false`) and zero `worker_paused` / `worker_resumed`;
    - with `pause` = 1 and `rc2Refill` = 0: only `worker_paused` / `worker_resumed` and zero `rest_toggled`.
    - Both families stay pinned. QA asserts the count in both flag configs (v5 ships the first).
  - **TEL-AC15 boost_used retired (D-PMW-2026-10-30-10; D-HOP-2026-10-30-35).**
    - No `boost_used` event is ever emitted in any flag config.
    - The Shift-sheet **Boost slot is visible in v5** iff `m4.flags.abilities` = 1 **and** `m4.flags.rc2Refill` = 1 (rc2 v1.3.3 L145, L157). A Boost logs exactly one `ability_used{in_sheet: true}` (abilities v1.3.5 L402) and no other event (no new event type).
    - `boost_used` isn't on the allowlist.
- **UI check:** none (data-only). The viewport_h boundary is checked at 360×739 and 360×740 in 3 engines together with SS-38.
- **AC7 row:** telemetry is outside the sim. AC7 hashes are byte-identical with telemetry emission on vs stubbed (seeds 1–200), so no sim RNG or state is touched (`sid` from `crypto`, L17).

Nothing on this card is measured until then. Per D-HOP-2026-10-30-37 A2, "The 18:00 deliverable is the best feasible candidate plus a trade-off table, and no bar is relaxed without my ruling." The trade-off table shows, for each constraint in the priority order below, where the candidate stands and what it gave up (owner: Econ).

On this card those are (1) the PL-AC4 (c) worst-seed dead zone, (2) PL-AC1, (3) PL-AC3 and (4) PL-AC6 (c). The order only says which constraint the candidate favours when they conflict. It relaxes no bar: a bar that the candidate misses still fails until HoP rules.

Econ 2026-10-30: s00-base was produced by `cand/tools/run.ts` (m5pl only, no parity run). Its per-seed `reach` and `noAffByF` maps equal `baseline/out/raw-<p>.json` (pl.ts, parity 0) on all 600 seed-runs (0 mismatches), so it is a valid before.
    - **Operating conditions (D-HOP-2026-11-10-07, QA-D-1110-BG54CERT):** (1) every analyze run of record runs on a copy of its raw dir, never in place (analyze writes `<dir>/metrics.json`); (2) before each compare, `sha256sum scratch/post-l10/cand/sweep/s00-base/metrics.json` must equal the sha256 above (compare.py hard-codes this mutable path as "before"); (3) any metrics.json byte pin records the exact command line that produced it (metrics.json embeds the `csv` path argument), or else the compare is on the JSON minus `csv`. Regeneration inputs: the 6 s00-base raws + `cand.csv`, sha256s in D-HOP-2026-11-10-07.
  - All of these are **scratch** numbers (`economy/gates.md` provenance rule).
- **Harness and measurement (shared by every AC):**
  - **Harness:** `scratch/post-l10/baseline/h/`, a copy of the #92 gate-3 harness `scratch/m51-pr/gates/h`.
    - Pinned files: `m5.ts` 29d423c5, `m5x.ts` 196e8ed5, `package-lock.json` 56569550.
    - `m5pl.ts` 11ff5046 and `pl.ts` 16ea5da9 only add per-level gap output. Parity was 0 mismatches over 600 seed-runs on a8c4e268.
    - **The harness of record for the final RC is TBD (Econ + Architect).** This one is scratch.
  - **Command:** run from `scratch/post-l10/baseline/h/`, one profile per run. Run them **serially, with at most 2 sims at a time on the shared box**:
    `CSV=<csv> SEEDS=200 S0=1 T=4000 PROFS=<optimal|seller15|casual> node node_modules/.bin/vite-node pl.ts > <dir>/raw-<p>.json 2> <dir>/raw-<p>.log`
    then `python3 /workspace/egg-farm-studio/scratch/post-l10/cand/tools/analyze.py <dir> <csv>` (writes `<dir>/metrics.json`; analyze.py sha256 `1764791afd482ac3ad223db8ab003f412538a0270fdbbc92d2cd0c49e1eea234` (sha1 8f6e4e9b; replacement per D-HOP-2026-11-10-07, QA-D-1110-BG54CERT; e67f184b unrecoverable))
    then `python3 /workspace/egg-farm-studio/scratch/post-l10/cand/tools/compare.py <dir> <dir>/compare.md <dir>/verdicts.json` (before = `sweep/s00-base`; compare.py sha256 `2100bd704e003f68fa3d4c8e5a5f0fe520caea5d8c9581da56f1c1381086e927` (sha1 2b85337d; replacement per D-HOP-2026-11-10-07, QA-D-1110-BG54CERT; 8a31fe7b unrecoverable)).
    - The final candidate runs on **`pl.ts`, not `cand/tools/run.ts`**. run.ts skips the m5.run parity check, and its own header says "the final candidate is re-run with pl.ts for parity". `parityMismatches` must be 0 in every `raw-<p>.log`. Econ 2026-10-30: pl.ts writes the field twice, as the stderr line `{"prof":p,"parityMismatches":n}` (the .log) and as `prof.<p>.parityMismatches` in the raw JSON. Both must be 0, and the raw JSON's `runner` must be `pl.ts (SCRATCH)`. run.ts writes a hardcoded `parityMismatches: 0`, and `cand/tools/analyze.py` checks neither field, so a run.ts output would look clean. The `runner` check is what catches it.
    - Don't use `cand/tools/sweep.sh` for evidence: it starts 6 sims in parallel.
  - **Archetypes (profiles):** optimal, seller15 and casual (D-HOP-11/-15; `PROFS` in `m5.ts`).
  - **Seeds:** 1-200. **T:** 4000 s. **Config:** flags-off `CFG none`, with every M5 mechanic off (D-HOP-2026-10-30-06; baseline README). PL-AC9 and the final-RC PL-AC4 (c) check use Econ's all-flags-on harness config instead (see "What blocks where").
  - analyze.py asserts that all 200 seeds reach L30 in every profile. A seed that doesn't is a FAIL, not a skip.
  - **Time on level L** = reach(L+1) − reach(L), in simulated seconds, per seed. reach(L) is the sim time at which the Farm first reaches level L (the `pacing-guards.ts` convention; baseline README "Definitions"). L30 is maxLevel, so it has no time on level. L29's time ends at reach(30). farm_cost row L is the cost of L→L+1. Econ 2026-10-30: confirmed in code. `m5pl.ts` sets reach(L) at the tick of the Farm purchase (check-in ticks, t rounded to 0.1 s). analyze.py computes `r[L+1] − r[L]` per seed, then takes the quantile over seeds.
  - **Quantiles** are nearest-rank over the 200 seeds: P50 is the 100th sorted value and P95 the 190th. Econ 2026-10-30: confirmed in code. analyze.py uses `q(a,p) = sorted(a)[ceil(p·n) − 1]`, the same as `quantile` in `tests/pacing-guards.ts`. At n = 200 that is index 99 (P50) and index 189 (P95), with no interpolation. "Median" means P50.
  - **Gap(L)**, "as pinned" (D-HOP-15: "The gap definition stays as pinned"): the longest nothing-affordable stretch, counting any purchasable row (harness `candidates()`), sampled each whole second and attributed to the Farm level at which it starts. Band gap = the per-seed max over the band's levels, then the quantile over seeds. Known conservative caveat: baseline README "Caveat" (a stretch is not closed if coins become affordable and are spent in the same tick).
- **ACs:**
  - **PL-AC1** (D-HOP-15 bar (1), **blocking**). Given the candidate CSV, run on the harness above, when each profile p and each L in 11..29 are measured, then P50 time on L ≤ 2.000 × P50 time on L−1.
    - Read from `metrics.json` `prof.<p>.level.<L>.tstep` ≤ 2; `bar_2x` is empty; `verdicts.json` `2x_P50` is empty.
    - That's 57 checks (19 levels × 3 profiles). One ratio above 2 is a FAIL. Econ 2026-10-30: `tstep` = round(P50(L) / P50(L−1), 3), so the test runs on the 3-decimal value (a raw ratio up to 2.0005 reads 2.0 and passes). The bar is the rounded value.
    - L11's denominator is L10's P50, and rows 0-10 don't change (PL-AC5).
    - Per-seed ratios (`seeds_over_2x`) are reported, not gated.
    - Before value (info): a8c4e268 fails casual L15 ×2.454 and L17 ×2.004 (tables.md).
    - Evidence: the sha256s of `metrics.json`, `compare.md` and `verdicts.json`, plus the raw JSON/log sha256s.
  - **PL-AC2** (D-HOP-15 bar (2), **blocking**). Given the same run, then for each p and each L in 11..29, P95 time on L ≤ 180.0 s.
    - Read from `tP95`; `bar_p95_180` is empty; `verdicts.json` `P95_180` is empty. That's 57 checks.
    - Worst-seed time (`tW`) is reported, not gated.
    - Before value (info, baseline README): a8c4e268 is over 180 s at optimal L17/20/21/27, seller15 L17/18/20/21/22/26/27, and casual L17/18/20-24/26/27.
    - Evidence: as PL-AC1.
  - **PL-AC3** (D-HOP-11 L10→L30 total, tightened by D-HOP-18, **blocking** flags-off; metric ruled by D-HOP-2026-10-30-37 (13:24, `product/decisions.md` L1815) D: "±10% uses the P50 of per-seed reach(30)−reach(10) against refs 2116.0/2100.2/2041.5 s (d6ac395c), and P10/P90 are also reported"; the d6ac395c ref is now `89014e8194bd92fde2c3083ad66804c23ef42ba1e7958295bfbf98c8a07264e1`, replacement per D-HOP-2026-11-10-07, QA-D-1110-BG54CERT; d6ac395c unrecoverable). Per profile, total = the P50 over seeds of reach(30) − reach(10) (`tot10_30_P50_s`). It passes when |total_after / total_before − 1| ≤ 0.10 (`verdicts.json` `total_pm10` is the pair [after/before − 1, pass]. Econ 2026-10-30: the pass flag is the second element, and it must be true).

The before values come from Econ's scratch `s00-base/metrics.json` (`89014e8194bd92fde2c3083ad66804c23ef42ba1e7958295bfbf98c8a07264e1`, replacement per D-HOP-2026-11-10-07, QA-D-1110-BG54CERT; d6ac395c unrecoverable): optimal **2116.0 s**, seller15 **2100.2 s**, casual **2041.5 s**. Econ 2026-10-30: verified on d6ac395c (now unrecoverable); HoQA 2026-11-10 (QA-D-1110-BG54CERT) re-verified the same values on the replacement. It records CSV sha256 a8c4e268…. `prof.<p>.tot10_30_P50_s` is 2116.0 (optimal), 2100.2 (seller15) and 2041.5 (casual), each the nearest-rank P50 over seeds 1-200 of reach(30) − reach(10), on flags-off `CFG none` with T 4000 s.
    - So the pass bands are about 1904.4–2327.6 s, 1890.2–2310.2 s and 1837.4–2245.7 s.

It runs on Econ's all-flags-on harness config, validated at freeze on the RC commit (config sha and validation record: TBD Econ at freeze, gap 5). The same metric on flags-off runs, including the final RC, is reported only. Econ 2026-10-30, exact metric:
        - **Gap per level:** for each profile p (optimal, seller15, casual) and each seed 1-200, gap(L) is the longest nothing-affordable stretch (whole-second samples, integer s) that starts while the Farm is at level L, for L = 11..29 (`noAffByF`).
        - **Band worst:** `gW` is the max over seeds 1-200 of the per-seed max over the band's levels.
        - **As one condition:** the 12 checks pass only if no (p, seed, L) with L in 11..29 has gap(L) > 180 s, i.e. `gap_seeds_over_180` is empty for all 3 profiles. This is a per-level, per-seed maximum. It is not a P95, and it is not the aggregate G4 `longestNoAff`.
        - **G4 is reported only:** G4 also counts stretches that start at F10 (L10 worst on a8c4e268: 55/40/55 s), so it can differ.
        - **Seeds over 180 s on a8c4e268:** optimal s158 L25 202 s, s113 L21 189 s, s102 L27 183 s, s49 L27 181 s. seller15 and casual have none.
        - **Band `gW` on a8c4e268** (F11-15/F16-20/F21-25/F26-30): optimal 118/162/202/183, seller15 76/97/98/153, casual 55/103/74/129.
        - **Closed by D-HOP-37 C** (was gap 12): flags-off is reported only, and the blocking run uses Econ's all-flags-on config validated at freeze on the RC commit.
      - **The before data already fails this.** On a8c4e268, optimal's worst is **202 s** (band F21-25, seed gap at L25; G4 any-row worst also 202 s). Optimal F26-30's worst is **183 s** (L27), and optimal's worst at L21 is 189 s (seed 113). Econ 2026-10-30 wording fix: the overall per-level worst is 202 s at L25 (seed 158). These are flags-off values. The blocking check is the all-flags-on final-RC run, and its before value isn't measured yet (gap 5). So the candidate must bring optimal's worst under 180 s on the all-flags-on final-RC run, or this AC fails.

This is the flags-off candidate target and constraint (1) in the D-HOP-37 A2 priority order. It is not the blocking run (D-HOP-37 C):
        1. band F21-25 worst (`gW`), 202 s at L25 → ≤ 180 s;
        2. band F26-30 worst (`gW`), 183 s at L27 → ≤ 180 s;
        3. per-level L21 worst (`level.21.gW`), 189 s → ≤ 180 s.

Econ 2026-10-30: `compare.md` and `metrics.json` don't contain row 30, because analyze.py reads rows 10–29. So (a), (b) and (d) are checked by `scratch/post-l10/gate/csvcheck.py`.
    - (b) "Step ≤×1.30": `farm_cost[L] / farm_cost[L−1]` ≤ 1.30 for L = 11..29. Row 30 is excluded: it is never buyable, and 624316/190000 = ×3.29 on a8c4e268. Econ 2026-10-30, definition:
      - **Step:** step(L) = farm_cost[L] / farm_cost[L−1] on the CSV list price, unrounded, for L = 11..29. That is 19 steps, and step(11) divides by the frozen row 10, 1,368. Equivalently, farm_cost[L+1] / farm_cost[L] for L = 10..28.
      - **Bar:** every step must satisfy 1.00 < step(L) ≤ 1.30.
      - **Row 30 excluded:** step(30) = 624,316 / farm_cost[29]. Row 30 is the L30→L31 cost, which is never buyable at maxLevel 30. On a8c4e268 it is ×3.29 (624,316 / 190,000). This is the known exception: D-HOP-11 pins L30, and it is on the M5.1 watch list. The card is right to exclude it. D-HOP-37 A1 confirms it: the L29→30 step is a watch-list exception, and steps are checked on L11–29 only. The L30 purchase stays inside the dead-zone check (PL-AC4 (c)).
    - (c) Milestone discounts at F15/F20/F25 are present: **a target, not a hard requirement** (D-HOP-37 A2: "milestone steps at L15/20/25 are a target, not a hard requirement"). Missing them doesn't fail the card. It is the lowest priority, (4), and the trade-off table reports it. Econ 2026-10-30, definition:
      - **No prior definition:** no CSV column, `economy.md` section, decision or sim code defines a Farm milestone discount. The CSV's milestone columns (`hens_ms*`, `egg_grade*`, `balanced*`) belong to the Hens and Egg-value tracks.
      - **No runtime discount:** the sim charges `farmCost(L)`, which is the CSV row (`src/sim/actions.ts` L29).
      - **Definition:** a milestone discount is a smaller list-price step on the first Farm purchase made at F15, F20 and F25. That means step(L) < the curve's default step for L = 15, 20 and 25, while still > 1.00.
      - **Size:** 1 − step(L) / default step, reported per milestone. Econ's scratch runs s02-ms120 and s03-ms110 used ×1.20 and ×1.10 against ×1.2999, about 8% and 15% off.
      - **Pass:** "present" means all three steps are below the default. The size is a candidate parameter, not a fixed bar. PMFU, per D-HOP-37 A2: since (c) is a target, the result is reported as met or not met. It doesn't fail the card.
      - **E-G9:** E-G9 (`economy/gates.md` L22, "Costs strictly rising, every track") applies to the CSV list price. With no runtime discount, the list price is also the price paid. A discount defined this way keeps costs strictly rising, so it cannot break E-G9.
      - **What would fail:** a step ≤ ×1.00 fails E-G9, PL-AC6 (b) and the build. `scripts/sync-economy.ts` L53–55 throws if farm_cost is not strictly increasing from L0 to maxLevel, and L189 does the same for hens_cost and egg_value_cost.
    - (d) There are no rows L31+ (out of scope; a8c4e268 has 31 rows).
